  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调查将终端智能体的行为分散在软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用等不同领域，缺乏统一框架来理解其核心的‘动作-观察’循环，导致系统架构、能力获取与评估之间的关联不清。

Method: 以‘终端命令执行-文本反馈-状态化环境交互’为组织主线，构建终端智能体系统架构（模型、接口、执行框架、运行时、环境）与七维终端能力画像，并通过固定条件诊断实验对基准族进行比较分析。

Result: 研究发现：实际行为由多组件共同塑造；可执行轨迹支撑基于行为后果的学习，但主流评估重最终结果而轻过程质量、恢复与治理；不同基准族暴露不同过程信号，组件归因具有基准依赖性。

Conclusion: 需在评估中显式报告系统与运行时条件，并辅以可重放轨迹和过程级证据；所提框架为软件工程及新兴领域中终端中介智能体的研究提供统一基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through terminals, yet existing surveys disperse terminal-mediated behavior across software engineering, tool use, and computer-use research. We regard terminal agents as systems whose dominant progress-bearing action--observation loop is mediated by terminal command execution, textual feedback, and stateful environment interaction. Using terminal-mediated execution as an organizing lens, this survey establishes workload-level boundaries and connects system architecture, competence acquisition, and evaluation through a seven-dimensional terminal competence profile. Our synthesis shows that realized behavior is jointly shaped by the model, interface, harness, runtime, and environment. Executable trajectories ground learning in action consequences, verification, and recovery, whereas prevailing evaluations emphasize final outcomes and expose process quality, recovery, and governance unevenly. Bounded fixed-condition diagnostics illustrate two implications: benchmark families expose different process signals, and matched system comparisons reveal benchmark-dependent performance and limits of component attribution. These findings motivate explicit reporting of system and runtime conditions, supported by replayable traces and process-level evidence. The framework provides a unified basis for studying terminal-mediated agency across software engineering and emerging application domains.

</details>


### [79] [Lost in Translation: How Universal Ethical Values Fail to Translate Across Global Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20490)
*Ozioma C. Oguine,Munachimso B. Oguine,Cesar Cervera,Jenny Yang,Pooja Voladoddi,Mario Rodriguez,Saif Eddin Bani Malhem,Karla Badillo-Urquiola,Daricia Wilkinson*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究通过访谈10个国家14位专家，揭示AI伦理框架在实际应用中存在结构性不平等与价值翻译差距，提出迈向多元治理的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理框架将公平、透明、问责等价值视为普适且可统一操作化，但忽略了不同社会文化背景下的实践差异，需审视其实际适用性。

Method: 对来自10个国家的14位专家进行质性访谈，分析其在AI实践中的价值解读、风险认知及治理设想，归纳出翻译差距与结构性条件。

Result: 发现隐私被视为集体关系性而非个体性、透明作为信任问责而非技术披露、公平强调获取与代表权而非结果均等；并识别出基础设施约束、剥削实践与技术神秘化等不平等条件。

Conclusion: AI伦理需从固定技术标准转向情境敏感的多元治理，重新分配知识权威，将伦理协商视为持续过程。

Abstract: AI ethics frameworks treat values such as fairness, transparency, and accountability as universal and uniformly operationalizable across contexts. We examined how 14 experts across 10 countries made sense of AI in practice, reinterpreted core values, and envisioned governance alternatives. We found that AI deployment is characterized by structurally unequal conditions, marked by infrastructural constraints, extractive practices, and a "mystification" of technology, which fundamentally shape perceptions of risks and opportunities. Our findings reveal that experts reinterpret values to fit local moral logics: privacy as collective and relational rather than individual; transparency as trust-building accountability rather than technical disclosure; and fairness as equity in access and representation rather than parity in outcomes. We identify these as translation gaps between encoded global frameworks and situated local practices. Finally, we propose pathways toward plural governance that redistributes epistemic authority and treats ethical negotiation as an ongoing, context-sensitive process rather than a settled technical standard.

</details>


### [80] [A Temporal Planning Approach for Intelligent Flood Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20510)
*Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个基于时间规划的智能洪水响应框架，支持多区域同时受灾时的行动调度、资源管理和执行中重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多区域同时洪水应对需要严格的时间顺序和资源约束，传统方法难以高效协调，需自动化规划支撑。

Method: 用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种语言建模完整洪水响应生命周期，涵盖分诊、路径可达性、资源分配、供应管理及动态重规划。

Result: 实验证明框架可行且可扩展，能有效建模和求解洪水场景，并给出规划器选择建议。

Conclusion: 时间规划适用于复杂洪水响应，框架在表达性和兼容性上有优势，可为实际应急系统提供参考。

Abstract: Effective response to multiple, simultaneously flooded areas requires coordinating appropriate actions in the correct temporal order, under severe resource constraints. Automated planning provides a foundation for addressing this challenge by generating time-aware schedules, given a formal description of available resources, constraints, and goals. This work presents an intelligent flood-response framework that exploits temporal planning and models the complete operational life cycle of flood response. The framework incorporates priority-driven triage, route accessibility and travel costs, resource allocation, and supply management, while also supporting mid-execution re-planning in response to unexpected environmental changes. The framework is formulated both in the Action Notation Modeling Language (ANML) and the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) 2.1, facilitating compatibility with a wider range of temporal planners. Experimental results establish the feasibility and scalability of the proposed framework, showing that flood response scenarios can be effectively modeled and solved using temporal planning, while providing guidance on planner selection.

</details>


### [81] [Volumetric Radiology AI in the Era of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20549)
*Zanting Ye,Shengyuan Liu,Xin Liu,Chenhui Wang,Zhisong Wang,Jiashuai Liu,Zipei Wang,Cheng Wang,Wentao Pan,Mengjie Fang,Di Dong,Mohammad Salmanpour,Arman Rahmim,Yu Gu,Yong Xia,Hongming Shan,Yixuan Yuan,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文综述了200余篇文献，系统分析了多模态大语言模型在容积放射学中的现状，提出需保留三维空间信息与代理式编排以弥合表示不匹配，并引入Claim-Design-Validation框架评估技术、工作流与临床声明的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs多基于2D图像或压缩表示，与容积放射学所需的完整3D空间上下文和定量信息存在根本性表示错配，且缺乏对系统级工作流集成与验证的系统梳理。

Method: 按模型级容积表示与多模态理解、系统级代理式编排两大主线组织文献，区分2D视图/报告推理与原生3D建模的适用场景，并提出Claim-Design-Validation评估框架。

Result: 发现原生容积建模与代理能力取决于任务的空间、定量、上下文及工作流需求；临床可信度依赖忠实容积表示、可追溯行为、对齐验证及明确人类监督。

Conclusion: 可靠的容积放射学AI需在模型层面保留3D信息、在系统层面实现可验证的代理式工作流集成，并通过声称-设计-验证对齐确保临床落地可信。

Abstract: Advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are extending radiological artificial intelligence (AI) beyond task-specific image analysis toward multimodal understanding and reasoning. Volumetric radiology, however, presents a fundamental representational mismatch: clinical interpretation often requires full-volume spatial context and acquisition-dependent quantitative information, whereas current MLLMs are commonly conditioned on selected two-dimensional (2D) images, compressed visual representations, or report-derived text. Reliable volumetric radiology AI therefore requires representations that preserve task-relevant three-dimensional (3D) information and systems that can access, verify, and integrate this information across clinical workflows. In this Review, we examine more than 200 publications through July 2026. We organize the literature around volumetric representation and multimodal understanding at the model level, agentic orchestration at the system level, and their links to clinical applications and evaluation. We review volumetric foundation models, language alignment and compression strategies, and agentic systems that extend MLLMs through planning, tools, memory, and workflow interaction. We distinguish settings in which selected 2D views or report-mediated reasoning may suffice from those that warrant native volumetric modeling. We also introduce a Claim-Design-Validation framework to assess whether technical, workflow, and clinical claims are matched by appropriate design and validation. Across the literature, native volumetric modeling and agentic capabilities depend on the spatial, quantitative, contextual, and workflow requirements of the intended task. Clinical credibility requires faithful volumetric representation, traceable system behavior, claim-aligned validation, and clearly defined human oversight in realistic workflows.

</details>


### [82] [Consilience: Conformally Calibrated Communication Control for Hidden-Profile Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20564)
*Abhijith Babu,Ramneet Kaur,Vishal Pramanik,Olivera Kotevska,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Susmit Jha,Sunny Raj,Yanzhao Wu,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Consilience框架，通过回合级共形校准为多智能体LLM通信提供分布无关的有限样本保证，在隐藏信息任务中提升决策准确率和通信效率，甚至超越全信息基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在隐藏信息场景下依赖通信协调，但现有固定或非结构化协议无法保证对话行为适当性，缺乏对通信动作的可靠性保障。

Method: 每回合用紧凑状态（不确定性、分歧、证据增益、冗余、过早共识）总结讨论，选择通信干预和发言者；核心是回合级共形校准，提供单步遗憾的有界保证，并通过接受机制替换不合格动作。

Result: 在12个开源/闭源模型的HiddenBench任务上，相比固定和非结构化协议，Consilience提升决策准确率和通信效率，有时超过所有智能体观察全部证据的基线。

Conclusion: 经认证的自适应通信控制比增加信息可用性更有价值，为可靠的多智能体LLM协调提供了实用机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can improve reasoning by pooling diverse perspectives, but their effectiveness depends on coordinating communication, particularly in hidden-profile settings where each agent holds only part of the evidence required for a correct decision. Existing protocols, including fixed schedules, round-robin exchange, and unstructured debate, provide no guarantee that a conversational action is appropriate. We propose Consilience, an inference-time orchestration framework that both steers and certifies multi-agent communication under distributed private information. At each turn, Consilience summarizes the discussion using a compact state capturing uncertainty, disagreement, evidence gain, redundancy, and premature consensus, then selects both a communication intervention (challenge, clarify, seek evidence, or route) and an appropriate speaker. Its central contribution is a round-wise conformal calibration procedure that provides a distribution-free, finite-sample guarantee: at each discussion round, conditional on reaching that round, the one-step regret of a controller's proposed action is bounded by a calibrated threshold with marginal probability at least 1 - alpha; an acceptance mechanism enforces the same guarantee for the executed action by replacing inadmissible proposals. On HiddenBench-style hidden-profile tasks spanning 12 open and closed weight language models, Consilience improves decision accuracy and communication efficiency over fixed and unstructured discussion protocols, sometimes surpassing a full-information baseline where every agent observes all evidence. These results demonstrate that certified adaptive communication control can be more valuable than increasing information availability, providing a practical mechanism for reliable multi-agent LLM coordination.

</details>


### [83] [Dual-Cache Latent Space Communication between Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20617)
*Jiyao Liu,Qi Zhang,Yaoyi Jia,Ziwen Kan,Song Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: XKV是一种新的跨模型KV缓存翻译方法，通过可学习查询注意力、跨层记忆混合和共享位置解码器，解决了现有方法在异构模型间通信的限制，实现更高效、更准确的智能体间信息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模型KV缓存翻译方法（如LCF-X）存在三个限制：仅压缩单一发送方缓存、缺乏联合跨层记忆、假设模型层数和KV几何结构匹配，导致在异构模型间通信效率低且准确性不足。

Method: XKV使用可学习查询注意力池化双方缓存，通过自注意力在接收方对齐的层令牌上混合池化摘要，并用可学习层映射适配不同深度；共享位置解码器允许每个原始接收方缓存位置以原生KV几何检索其逐头门控残差。

Result: 在45个数据集-模型对设置中，XKV取得最高宏观分数和最佳平均排名，在ROPES上比LCF-X提升4.6 EM和4.2 F1；训练参数减少76%，缓存对翻译速度快10.3倍，端到端比LCF-X快26%，比文本通信快6.8倍。

Conclusion: XKV有效克服了现有跨模型协议的限制，为异构多智能体LLM系统提供了一种高效、通用且性能优越的通信方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems split work across models, so answering often requires knowledge that sits in another agent's context: a Sharer has encoded information that a Receiver needs to complete its task. They usually communicate by exchanging text, which puts autoregressive decoding on the critical path and reduces the exchange to a discrete message written without sight of the receiver's state. Recent latent protocols instead translate the sharer's key-value (KV) cache into the receiver's: C2C supports heterogeneous models but requires both to read the same input, while LCF-X removes this shared-context requirement through position-free sharer-cache pooling. Three restrictions remain: LCF-X compresses the sharer alone, supplies the same layer-local summary to every receiver position with no joint cross-layer memory to retrieve from, and assumes matched layer count and KV geometry. We introduce XKV, which lifts all three: learned-query attention pools both caches; self-attention over receiver-aligned layer tokens, with a learned layer map reconciling different depths, mixes the pooled summaries into a compact joint memory; and a shared position decoder lets every raw receiver cache position retrieve its own per-head-gated residual in the receiver's native KV geometry. Both models stay frozen and may differ in family, depth, KV-head count, head dimension, and tokenizer; only the translator is trained. Across 45 dataset-model-pair settings (six heterogeneous and three same-model ordered pairings, five datasets), XKV attains the highest macro score and best average rank, improving on LCF-X on every dataset (by 4.6 exact-match and 4.2 F1 points on ROPES) and surpassing text communication on four of the five, while training 76% fewer parameters and translating a cache pair 10.3x faster (5.8 vs. 59.9 ms); end to end, XKV is 26% faster than LCF-X and 6.8x faster than text communication.

</details>


### [84] [Hidden Axis of Uncertainty: Latent-Posterior Alignment in Graph Neural Networks with Bayesian Output Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20758)
*Suk Hoon Choi,Damdae Park,Junhyuk Choi,Hyein Jung,Changsoo Kim,Ung Lee,Kyeongsu Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文发现图神经网络中贝叶斯输出层的预测不确定性下降并非源于后验收缩，而是由潜在表示与低方差后验方向的几何对齐（LPA）驱动，并据此提出对齐引导学习（AGL）以有效降低不确定性并改善校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论将贝叶斯神经网络的不确定性降低归因于后验收缩，但其假设在深度模型中未必成立；理解GNN中不确定性形成的真实机制对可靠预测至关重要。

Method: 通过干预实验验证LPA现象，提出Alignment-Guided Learning (AGL)在训练中显式促进潜在表示与后验低方差方向的几何对齐。

Result: AGL在保持精度的同时有效降低预测不确定性，并改善结构校准（模型置信度与数据密度更一致）。

Conclusion: 不确定性动态应关注潜在空间与参数空间的几何交互而非后验幅度，为GNN贝叶斯输出层提供了新视角。

Abstract: Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) with Bayesian output layers provide a principled and tractable framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty, yet the mechanisms shaping that uncertainty remain unclear. While conventional theory attributes uncertainty reduction to posterior contraction, the corresponding assumptions need not hold for deep models. In the Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) with Bayesian output layers studied here, we observe that predictive uncertainty decreases as latent representations shift toward lower-variance posterior directions, even though the posterior variance does not contract. We term this behavior Latent-Posterior Alignment (LPA) and conduct interventional experiments that support its functional role in shaping predictive uncertainty. Building on this insight, we propose Alignment-Guided Learning (AGL), which explicitly promotes this alignment during training. AGL effectively reduces predictive uncertainty while preserving accuracy and improves structural calibration, ensuring that the model confidence faithfully mirrors underlying data density. These findings provide a new perspective on uncertainty dynamics in GNNs with mean-field Bayesian output layers, shifting the focus from the magnitude of the posterior to the geometric interplay between latent and parameter spaces.

</details>


### [85] [Applying Anthropic Primitives at Large Enterprises: Harness Paradigm for Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20622)
*George Juraj Salapa*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种编码代理的‘脚手架’范式，使同一套代码在多种部署场景中无需修改即可运行，并通过将审计简化为读取指令文件来大幅降低维护和审查成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型降低了编写自定义代码的成本，但审查和维护成本未降低，且企业级集中治理方案（如产品、图编排框架、低代码平台）皆为定制且范围有限，无法兼顾灵活性与统一治理。

Method: 基于‘脚手架’范式构建统一架构：一个未修改的脚手架作为主干，通过凭证作用域工具、将授权逻辑置于脚手架之外、以及将注册作为推送代码的副作用等四种机制，实现跨部署一致性。

Result: 该架构在任务层面有效，优于更复杂的架构，且脚手架选择对基准结果差异的影响大于模型选择，缩小了研究成果与企业采用之间的治理差距。

Conclusion: 脚手架范式提供了一种可治理、可审计且无需每次定制的新选择，有望替代当前企业中的定制化集中治理方案。

Abstract: Frontier models have collapsed the cost of writing custom code: a niche problem a specialist sees in their own domain now costs an afternoon. The cost of reviewing and maintaining that code hasn't collapsed. Each solution drifts from the next; understanding one means reading its codebase from scratch. Large enterprises build something centrally governed instead: at worst an off-the-shelf product, at best a graph-orchestration framework wired bespoke per use case, or a low-code platform used as the orchestrator. These are custom every time and limited in scope. Enterprises don't weigh a third option that escapes both constraints: the harness paradigm.
  Recent work treats the coding-agent harness as enterprise infrastructure rather than a coding tool, converging on three findings: harnesses suffice at the task level and outperform more elaborate architectures on enterprise work (arXiv:2604.00073, arXiv:2604.13107); harness choice accounts for most of the variance in agent benchmark results, more than model choice does (arXiv:2605.23950); and the gap between that finding and enterprise adoption is governance (arXiv:2605.10223, arXiv:2605.18747).
  We propose an architecture that closes that gap. One harness runs unmodified as the backbone; the code stays identical across every deployment, so reviewing what gets built collapses to reading its instructions file. Section 4 gives four mechanisms: credential-scoped tooling, where each backend gets one generic request tool and a scoped credential instead of a hand-built method; authorization logic outside the harness, so one artifact runs as a cron backbone, a chat-surface engine, and a terminal tool; registration is a side effect of pushing code, collapsing an audit a review of a text file.
  Built on microcc (<https://pypi.org/project/micro-cc/>), our reference harness.

</details>


### [86] [Auditable by Construction: An Ontology-Driven Framework for Trustworthy LLM Analytics in Enterprise Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20661)
*Sergiy Lunyakin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出知识驱动分析框架（KDAF），通过本体构建与上下文感知相关性传播（CARP）实现可审计的检索增强生成；在FinanceBench上，KDAF在答案正确性上与基线无显著差异，但在引文可追溯性上显著领先，证明可审计性而非准确性才是本体检索的价值所在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业金融中大语言模型的应用受信任约束而非流畅性限制，FP&A等受监管流程要求答案可溯源、可审计；现有RAG评估仅关注准确性，忽视了可审计性，需建立兼顾二者的评估体系。

Method: 提出KDAF框架，包含六个迭代阶段构建本体驱动知识系统；检索采用CARP，传播证据时保留关系类型、置信度与来源谱系；在145个FinanceBench问题上对比零上下文、BM25、概念加权词汇检索及无约束图遍历，统计检验差异并显示置信区间。

Result: 零上下文正确率仅4.1%，检索条件为10-12%；KDAF与BM25正确率差为-0.007（CI含零），统计无差异；但KDAF引文可追溯性F1=0.515，超过无约束遍历+0.027和BM25+0.052，区间不含零；图检索无越界证据（0/426），词汇基线为16.8%和20.2%，且所有条目有完整来源链。

Conclusion: 在准确率维度上结构化检索未显优势（负面结果明确报告），但在可审计性维度上本体检索显著占优；企业金融应优先以可审计性作为RAG评估与选型的关键指标，而准确性不足以为结构化检索正名。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of large language models in finance is constrained less by fluency than by trust: in Financial Planning and Analysis (FP&A) and other regulated workflows, an answer is usable only if it is traceable to authoritative sources and auditable after the fact. This paper argues that retrieval-augmented generation for enterprise finance should be evaluated on auditability alongside accuracy, and presents the Knowledge-Driven Analytics Framework (KDAF), which builds ontology-driven knowledge systems through six iterative stages and retrieves evidence via Context-Aware Relevance Propagation (CARP), so that every retrieved fact carries its relationship type, confidence, and source lineage.
  An evaluation on FinanceBench (145 questions) compares KDAF against zero-context inference, BM25, concept-weighted lexical retrieval, and ungrounded graph traversal. First, retrieval is necessary: zero-context inference reaches 4.1% correctness against 10-12% for retrieval-augmented conditions. Second, on answer correctness the retrieval conditions are statistically indistinguishable (KDAF vs BM25: -0.007, 95% CI [-0.021, 0.000]), so accuracy alone does not justify structured retrieval here -- a negative result we report explicitly. Third, on auditability the ordering reverses: KDAF attains the highest citation traceability F1 (0.515), exceeding ungrounded traversal by +0.027 (CI [0.006, 0.050]) and BM25 by +0.052 (CI [0.024, 0.083]), intervals excluding zero. Graph-structured retrieval also admits no evidence from outside the question subject entity (0 of 426 items, against 16.8% and 20.2% for lexical baselines), and every selected item resolves to a complete provenance chain. We argue that auditability, not accuracy, is the axis on which ontology-grounded retrieval earns its cost.

</details>


### [87] [Decoupling Policy Extraction for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20909)
*Xuyao Lin,Yixiang Shan,Jinru Duan,Tao Yang,Xinyu Zhao,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦策略提取范式：离线RL中仅用行为克隆训练actor，推理时用独立critic对多个候选动作重排序，从而避免actor与critic耦合训练带来的OOD过估计和保守性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线RL联合训练actor和critic存在两个挑战：1）actor更新会漂移到高价值但OOD的动作并放大critic过估计；2）保守值估计或行为克隆正则化需要在抑制OOD动作和选择高价值动作之间艰难权衡。由于离线数据固定，actor策略改进无法产生新数据来验证或纠正critic。

Method: 将策略改进从actor训练中解耦：actor仅建模行为分布（行为克隆），推理时用单独训练的critic对多个actor生成的候选动作进行重排序，选出高价值动作作为最终策略。

Result: 实验表明该解耦范式优于行为克隆和联合训练的离线RL方法，且即使使用朴素Q-learning critic（无保守正则化）也保持有效。

Conclusion: 解耦策略提取是离线RL中一种有效且鲁棒的替代范式，通过将行为采样与价值选择分离，避免了耦合训练带来的过估计和保守性权衡问题。

Abstract: Offline RL methods commonly jointly train the actor and critic, where the critic is used to guide the actor toward higher-value actions. This coupled learning process is well motivated in online RL, where an improved actor collects new data that can further update the actor and the critic. However, training data remains fixed in offline RL, making actor-side policy improvement unable to generate new data to validate or correct the critic. Moreover, retaining this coupled paradigm leads to two related challenges. Firstly, actor updates can drift toward high-valued but potentially out-of-distribution (OOD) actions and amplify critic overestimation. Secondly, conservative value estimation or behavior-cloning regularization creates a difficult trade-off between suppressing OOD actions and selecting high-value actions within the data-supported region. Motivated by this observation, we revisit the conventional offline RL paradigm and propose decoupling policy improvement from actor training. Specifically, we train the actor solely to model the behavior distribution and perform policy improvement at inference time by reranking multiple actor-generated proposals with a separately learned critic. We refer to this paradigm as the decoupled policy extraction paradigm. Under such paradigm, the actor provides behavior-supported action candidates, while the critic performs value-based selection within this candidate set. Extensive experiments show that the decoupled policy extraction paradigm outperforms both behavior cloning and jointly learned offline RL methods, while remaining effective even with a naive Q-learning critic.

</details>


### [88] [DreamBench-SWE: A Multi-Session Memory-Hygiene Benchmark for Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20664)
*Sarthak Singh*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: DreamBench-SWE是一个多会话软件代理记忆卫生基准，后续任务依赖先前会话的非推断证据；后续审计完成720个单元，发现无外部记忆通过率最低，而特定托管Mem0配置最高，但未能确立外部系统机制或普遍优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未充分测试软件代理在多会话中的记忆卫生，即后续任务需利用早期会话的隐性证据；该基准通过可执行隐藏oracle评分，以区分不同记忆配置的实际效能，并弥补原始研究中的局限。

Method: 采用两折设计：原始缩放v2和预先注册的v2.1审计（后研究冻结，结果检查前定型）；测试四种条件：无外部记忆、确定性逐字事件记忆、类型加原始参考探针、固定托管Mem0字面存储配置；评分由隐藏oracle执行，共360个工单和720个S3单元。

Result: 无外部记忆通过率0.1167，逐字记忆0.4556，参考探针0.4611，Mem0配置0.5389；注册对比中，可用比较经Holm校正后均显著拒绝无记忆；主要机制对比因依从性拒绝而不可用；字面存储与逐字记忆的次要比较非确认性且敏感。

Conclusion: 审计支持DreamBench-SWE作为有区分度的可执行配置基准，并表征了一个特定托管记忆配置；但未提供外部系统机制的因果证据、记忆条件间优越性、等价性或产品广泛通用性；原始v2.0.5结果和工件保持未变。

Abstract: DreamBench-SWE is a multi-session benchmark for software-agent memory hygiene in which later software tasks depend on non-inferable evidence from earlier sessions and are scored by executable hidden oracles. We report the original scaled v2 fold and a separately preregistered v2.1 successor audit designed after that study but frozen before successor outcome inspection. The successor run completed 360/360 work units and 720/720 S3 cells across four conditions. In the original fold, the primary DF-hybrid--B5 contrast was null (95/180 versus 89/180; clustered p=.518, Holm p=1), not evidence of equivalence, and C9/C10 retained B0-headroom limitations. In the successor, no external memory achieved 21/180 passes (rate 0.1167), deterministic verbatim event memory 82/180 (rate 0.4556), the typed-plus-raw reference probe 83/180 (rate 0.4611), and one pinned hosted Mem0 literal-storage configuration 97/180 (rate 0.5389). The registered six-slot Family A retained unavailable slots at p=1; all three available comparisons against no memory rejected after Holm correction. Both preregistered mechanism contrasts were unavailable after pre-evaluation conformance rejection. The secondary literal-storage-versus-verbatim comparison was nonconfirmatory and sensitivity-dependent, while the comparison with the reference probe did not reject. The audit therefore supports DreamBench-SWE as a discriminating executable profile benchmark and characterizes one exact hosted-memory configuration, but it does not establish an external-system mechanism, superiority among memory-bearing conditions, equivalence, or broad product generality. The original v2.0.5 findings and artifacts remain unchanged.

</details>


### [89] [Training, learning and inference: unified dynamics of neural systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20965)
*Mian Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出原子生成事实f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho)及其编译得到的生成事实图（GFG），作为AI原生的可编译科学事实基板，保存生成历史。基于GFG构建递归科学流程，并以nanoGPT建立统一训练-学习动力学，提出二阶预测器（准确率91.43%，宏平均召回率91.49%），并将推理视为训练-学习动力学的冻结投影，在ResNet和扩散模型上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学事实难以保留生成历史，导致分析、干预和验证缺乏可追溯性；训练与学习缺乏统一动力学框架，推理与训练割裂，难以解释能力形成与衰退。

Method: 定义原子生成事实f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho)记录来源、变换、生成结果及关系角色，编译为GFG；基于GFG建立递归科学过程；在nanoGPT上提出训练为参数-优化器系统的状态演化，学习为功能支持的重组，利用三个主坐标（目标边界状态、目标特定更新几何、参数-Adam接收状态）构建二阶预测器；推理作为训练-学习动力学的冻结投影，组件门控与回滚揭示因果招募与非线性组合；在ResNet/CIFAR-100和扩散/CIFAR-10上验证跨架构泛化。

Result: 在保留运行上，二阶预测器对四种转换的准确率为91.43%，宏平均召回率为91.49%；控制反馈显示可能的双刃强化效应；ResNet/CIFAR-100和扩散/CIFAR-10实验证实了接收状态条件响应、持久支持重组和冻结推理投影的泛化性。

Conclusion: GFG和统一训练-学习动力学提供了一个可编译、可追溯的AI科学范式，为解释能力形成、干预和推理提供统一机制，并在多种模型上得到验证，但需要进一步探索双刃效应和更复杂任务。

Abstract: We define an atomic generation fact f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho), recording the origin, realized transformation, concrete occurrence, generated result and relation role. Compiled into a Generation-Fact Graph (GFG), these facts provide an AI-native, compilable scientific fact substrate preserving generation histories. We establish a GFG-based recursive scientific process in which analysis, intervention, replay and validation form facts for later cycles. Using nanoGPT, we establish unified training-learning dynamics. Training is the evolution of a parameter-optimizer system with state and memory: each actual training action enters the receiving state and produces a finite-amplitude nonlinear functional response conditioned by that state and target-specific update geometry. Learning is the persistent reorganization of distributed functional support by these responses; capability formation, maintenance, decline or recovery becomes observable when target-specific states are evaluated against their readout boundaries. Three primary coordinates - target-boundary state, target-specific update geometry and parameter-Adam receiving state - yield a second-order predictor operating before post-update outputs are read. On held-out runs, it achieved 91.43% accuracy and 91.49% macro-averaged recall across four transitions. We further establish inference as a frozen projection of training-learning dynamics. Component gating and rollback show causal recruitment and non-additive combination of query-conditioned support formed during training, deriving organizational conditions realized by Attention. Controlled feedback indicates possible double-edged reinforcement effects. ResNet/CIFAR-100 and diffusion/CIFAR-10 experiments confirm receiving-state-conditioned responses, persistent support reorganization and frozen inference projection beyond nanoGPT.

</details>


### [90] [Why2Speak: Faithful Reasoning for Abstaining Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20670)
*Shreya Mendi,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体在行动与放弃之间的决策中，暴露推理过程是否会改变其策略，发现能力与可审计性之间存在权衡，且标准忠实性评估方法可能高估推理的透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统需在行动与放弃间反复选择，而忠实推理对监督至关重要，但暴露推理可能改变被审计的策略，这一问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 基于Qwen3-8B，在多方对话干预时机任务中，对比直接策略、推理策略、监督微调和强化学习，并利用激活探针和行为消融评估推理的忠实性。

Result: 直接策略性能更强但无推理痕迹；推理策略性能较低；监督微调无法提升决策质量；强化学习同样失败，且发现群体相对目标在自信错误样本上无学习信号；标准忠实性方法存在过估问题。

Conclusion: 暴露推理会改变智能体的行为策略而非仅使其可观察，研究提供了评估可行动或放弃智能体推理监督的控制方法。

Abstract: Many agentic systems must repeatedly choose between acting and abstaining, making faithful reasoning important for oversight: an explanation is useful only if it reflects the computation that produced the action. We study this problem through intervention timing in multi-party conversation, where an assistant must decide whether to speak or remain silent. This setting exposes class imbalance, asymmetric action costs, and the possibility that exposing reasoning changes the policy being audited. Using Qwen3-8B, decoded with or without chain-of-thought reasoning, we compare direct decision policies, reasoning policies, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. We find a capability-auditability tradeoff: the strongest direct policy achieves higher quality but exposes no reasoning to inspect, while the reasoning policy provides a trace at the cost of lower performance, particularly recall of true intervention opportunities. Supervised fine-tuning either suppresses reasoning or preserves it without improving decision quality, while reinforcement learning also fails to improve the reasoning policy. We identify one mechanism underlying this failure: group relative objectives provide no learning signal on confidently wrong prompts when sampled rollouts all select the same action. Controlled activation probes and behavioral ablations show that standard faithfulness methods can overstate evidence that exposed reasoning reflects the underlying decision process. Probability-based metrics saturate under confident decisions, probes are vulnerable to class imbalance and textual leakage, and reasoning ablations can confound reasoning content with changes in inference mode. Together, these results show that exposing reasoning can change an agent's action policy rather than simply make it observable. We provide controls for evaluating reasoning-based oversight of agents that can act or abstain.

</details>


### [91] [CDRL: Certification-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Neutrino Flavor Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20686)
*Piyush Jha,Jake Rudolph,Victoria Knapp-Pérez,Max Fieg,Aishik Ghosh,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出认证驱动强化学习（CDRL），利用符号推理工具的结构化反馈将失败原因转化为可复用约束，在粒子物理中实现更高效的模型搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法依赖标量奖励，无法提供候选解失败的具体原因，导致智能体反复探索无效区域，搜索效率低下。

Method: CDRL在候选违反领域约束时，从符号推理工具获取证书识别导致失败的动作，并将证书转化为可复用约束，消除无效解类别并引导搜索。

Result: 在三个理论空间中，有效模型率提升至1.95倍，中微子模型率提升至6.33倍，评估候选数减少至1/4；提取的规则作为软约束可额外带来2倍有效率和3倍发现率增益。

Conclusion: CDRL能发现组合搜索空间中的可复用结构，为科学模型发现提供通用框架。

Abstract: Many scientific discovery problems require searching combinatorial hypothesis spaces under complex domain constraints. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising approach, but existing methods rely on scalar rewards that provide limited information about why candidate solutions fail, leading agents to repeatedly explore invalid regions. We introduce Certification-Driven Reinforcement Learning (CDRL), a framework that leverages structured feedback from symbolic reasoning tools. When a candidate violates domain constraints, these tools produce certificates identifying the actions responsible for failure. CDRL converts these certificates into reusable constraints that eliminate classes of invalid solutions and guide exploration toward valid regions. We evaluate CDRL on neutrino flavor model discovery in theoretical particle physics, where the hypothesis space exceeds $10^{26}$ possible models, and compare it with the state-of-the-art RL approach previously used for this task. Across three theory spaces, CDRL achieves up to 1.95$\times$ higher valid model rates and up to 6.33$\times$ higher neutrino model rates while evaluating up to 4$\times$ fewer candidates. We further extract 40 interpretable rules from search trajectories using a post-hoc decision-tree framework and show that reusing them as soft constraints yields gains of up to 2$\times$ in valid model rates and 3$\times$ in neutrino model discovery across all three theory spaces. These results suggest that CDRL uncovers reusable structure in combinatorial search spaces and provides a general framework for scientific model discovery.

</details>


### [92] [DirEAG: Dirichlet Evidence Aggregation for Calibrating Verbalized Confidence in Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20717)
*Haorui Xu,Yuzhou Zhu,Liyuan Gao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DirEAG，将多个置信度提示下的答案-置信度观测转化为软证据进行聚合，以提升大语言模型数学推理的置信度校准，同时保持答案选择竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒口头化置信度难以校准，现有方法忽视置信度的数值意义或无法学习提示与任务相关偏差。

Method: Dirichlet证据聚合：将每次 elicited 的答案-置信度观测转化为校准软证据，覆盖候选答案及附加空状态，用于表示无正确答案的情形。

Result: 在GSM8K、SVAMP和GSM-Hard上使用Qwen、Mistral和Gemma验证，相比直接平均和启发式聚合，DirEAG通常校准更好且答案选择相当；消融表明证据聚合与最终二值校准解决不同校准问题。

Conclusion: DirEAG为黑盒模型提供更可靠的不确定性估计，且证据聚合和二元校准互补，共同提升校准性能。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimation is essential for using large language models in mathematical reasoning, but black-box verbalized confidence is difficult to calibrate. When the same problem is queried under multiple confidence-steering prompts, the resulting answer-confidence observations contain useful uncertainty information, yet their scales may shift across steering levels, models, and datasets. Existing black-box uncertainty methods often rely on answer agreement, sample consistency, or entropy, which describe output variation but do not model the numerical meaning of self-reported confidence. Conversely, direct averaging or heuristic aggregation of elicited confidence cannot learn prompt- and task-dependent bias. We propose DirEAG, a Dirichlet Evidence Aggregation method that converts each elicited answer-confidence observation into calibrated soft evidence over generated candidate answers and an additional null state, allowing the model to represent cases where none of the candidates is correct. Experiments on GSM8K, SVAMP, and GSM-Hard with Qwen, Mistral, and Gemma models show that, compared with direct confidence averaging and heuristic confidence-steering aggregation, DirEAG often achieves better calibration while maintaining competitive answer selection. Ablations further reveal that evidence aggregation and final binary calibration address distinct parts of the calibration problem.

</details>


### [93] [Free-Probability Kernels for Zero-Rollout Hyperparameter Selection in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20998)
*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自由概率理论的确定性核函数，用于无回放地选择储层计算超参数，在多个基准上以极少的回放预算达到与穷举搜索相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算的高效性在超参数选择阶段被破坏，因为增益、输入尺度和泄漏率等参数需要多次回放调优，而验证回放成本高昂。

Method: 利用自由概率推导泄漏线性储层的交叉滞后传播系数，在宽极限下构造确定性时间核，通过核岭回归在短标记序列上对候选超参数排序，无需实例化或回放储层。

Result: 在十个合成基准上零回放选择达到0.772平均部署分数（穷举为0.774），节省156600次回放；小预算下用4.8%回放成本达到穷举参考；ETT三个数据集和蜂窝流量预测中以少量回放追平或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为确定性替代模型，在验证回放稀缺时有效选择储层运行状态，显著降低计算成本且性能损失极小。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [94] [Calibrating Criterion Revision in LLM Agents: Failure Modes and a Trace-Anchored Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20729)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对语言模型智能体‘标准修订’（criterion revision）的归因测量框架CMB-0.1，并通过12个跨域案例和4种实验臂的84次确定性评分试验发现，没有任何模型试验同时满足全部五个非补偿性条件，但这一零结果主要反映测量工具的校准问题而非模型能力缺失。基于诊断，作者设计了更严谨的CMB-0.4协议，作为未来测试的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体在失败后可能改进，或在跨片段时携带文本而不修改成功标准，但缺乏对‘标准修订’这一特定归因问题的严格测量。特别是当初始标准K0接受了违反更广泛承诺B的结果时，如何从观测上判断系统确实形成并持续使用了新标准K1，需要可操作、非补偿性的判定条件。

Method: 提出五个不可补偿条件：标准失败检测、模型提出的修订提案、新片段迁移、对声称载体的干预敏感性、以及持续性保持。构建CMB-0.1测量链，包含七种机制装置和四种局部量化工件，在十二个跨域案例上运行四种实验臂（无状态推理、仅追加历史、模型生成但由框架提交的状态、评估者编写的标准状态），共产生84次确定性评分器试验、96次调用和192次模型-案例-臂组合试验。

Result: 没有任何模型试验满足全部五个条件，但该零结果表明测量工具存在校准问题而非模型普遍无能。具体诊断包括：11次调用在一次重试后仍无效；若干承诺泄露了目标区分；框架执行了提交操作；删除操作复用了无状态调用；冲突修改涉及多个因素。Qwen2.5-7B模型在所有迁移和保持项目上均作答，但未使用修订状态，暴露了零状态重建问题。

Conclusion: 本文结果属于仪器校准而非模型排名。作者推导了前瞻性的、以轨迹为锚的CMB-0.4协议，要求隐藏迁移、显式WRITE/NO-WRITE/ESCALATE操作、独立记录的策略选择提交、匹配干预、重复隐藏项目和冻结的可执行标准器。该协议是后续设计而非已完成的确证结果。论文贡献在于测量链、首个实现的经验诊断，以及更精确的未来标准修订测试协议。

Abstract: Language-model agents can improve after failure or carry text across episodes without revising what counts as success. We study the narrower attribution problem of criterion revision: when criterion K0 accepts an outcome violating a broader commitment B, what observations justify saying that the system formed and persistently used K1? We require five non-compensatory conditions: criterion-failure detection, a model-emitted proposal, new-episode transfer, intervention sensitivity on the claimed carrier, and preservation.
  We evaluate CMB-0.1 on twelve cross-domain cases and four arms: stateless inference, append-only history, model-generated but harness-committed state, and evaluator-written oracle state. Seven mechanism fixtures yield 84 deterministic scorer trials; four local quantized artifacts yield 96 calls and 192 model-case-arm trials. No model trial satisfies all five conditions, but this zero does not establish general capability absence. Eleven calls remain invalid after one retry; several commitments disclose the target distinction; the harness performs commits; deletion reuses a stateless call; and conflict changes multiple factors. Qwen2.5-7B answers every transfer and preservation item without revision state, exposing zero-state reconstruction.
  These failures make CMB-0.1 an instrument-calibration result rather than a model ranking. We derive a prospective, trace-anchored CMB-0.4 protocol requiring concealed transfer, explicit WRITE/NO-WRITE/ESCALATE actions, a separately logged policy-selected commit, matched interventions, repeated hidden items, and a frozen executable oracle. It is a successor design, not a completed confirmatory result. The paper contributes a measurement chain, an empirical diagnosis of its first implementation, and a more discriminating protocol for future tests of criterion revision.

</details>


### [95] [ForeTime-VLA: Causal Future-Token Distillation from a World Action Model for Conveyor-Belt Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20735)
*Siyuan Ma,Yutian Zhang,Boshi Zhang,Qinglian Wu,Jiaqi Zhai,Dong Wei,Xiaojin Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ForeTime-VLA，通过从Fast-WAM教师蒸馏未来感知表征，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升动态操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略仅基于当前观测，缺乏对未来接触事件的预测能力；而世界模型虽能预测动态，但推理成本过高。

Method: 离线将当前与未来视频潜在表征压缩为白化64维目标，在线用八帧历史编码器预测该目标及操作相位和归一化转移时间。用四个未来token和一个相位token条件化VLM前缀，预测的未来和转移视界条件化动作专家。训练保留流匹配动作目标并添加余弦、关系几何、相位、时间转移和动作等价损失。

Result: 测试MAE降低2.63%，L2降低3.02%，推理延迟增加2.46-2.93%。真实机器人抓取成功率在静止和慢速场景分别达81.1%和58.9%，大幅超越基线；快速场景抓取数从2/30提升至11/30。

Conclusion: 因果未来token蒸馏是提升动态操作性能的有效方法，无需部署世界模型教师，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Manipulating moving objects requires a policy to anticipate contact events, yet vision-language-action (VLA) policies are commonly fine-tuned from the current observation alone. World action models (WAMs) learn predictive dynamics, but running a video-scale teacher or explicitly imagining future frames at deployment is costly. We introduce ForeTime-VLA, a dense pi0.5 policy that distills a future-aware, action-equivalent representation from a frozen Fast-WAM-derived teacher while remaining causal at inference. Offline, current and future video latents are compressed into a whitened 64-D target. Online, an eight-frame history encoder predicts this target together with manipulation phase and normalized time-to-transition. Four future tokens and one phase token condition the VLM prefix, while the predicted future and transition horizon condition the action expert. Training retains the original flow-matching action target and adds cosine, relational geometry, phase, time-to-transition, and action-equivalence objectives. On a deduplicated conveyor-belt dataset, we compare 40k-step checkpoints on 768 matched windows per split. Test MAE decreases from 0.134119 to 0.130593 (2.63%; paired-bootstrap 95% CI: 0.82-4.48% improvement), and test L2 decreases by 3.02%, at a 2.46-2.93% latency cost. In quantitative real-robot evaluation, ForeTime-VLA achieves 81.1% stationary and 58.9% slow-moving grasp success, exceeding the next-best reference by 12.2 and 22.2 percentage points, respectively. Across three belt speeds, it completes 44/90 grasps versus 23/90 for pi0.5, including 11/30 versus 2/30 at fast speed. The agreement between offline orientation gains and reduced real-robot contact-pose failures supports causal future-token distillation as an effective way to improve dynamic manipulation without deploying the world-model teacher.

</details>


### [96] [Designing a Robust LLM-Based Evaluation System for Agentic AI in Drug Discovery Through Human Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21057)
*Emma Granqvist,Rocío Mercado,Samuel Genheden*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个面向药物发现智能体ChatInvent的LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架，通过人类对齐研究优化裁判模型，实现可扩展且与专家一致的系统输出质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考指标（如BLEU/ROUGE）无法捕捉语义正确性，而人工评估无法跟上智能体系统的迭代速度；尽管LLM裁判范式可扩展，但现有药物发现基准未验证其与人类专家的一致性。

Method: 定义四个质量维度（完整性、相关性、结构清晰度、范围遵循）及确定性工具调用正确性检查；通过五名专家标注员对四种候选LLM裁判进行人类对齐验证；使用少样本人类标注示例优化最佳裁判模型。

Result: 优化后裁判与人类多数投票的一致性从0.80提升至0.86；在70个保留问题上发现非正式措辞不会系统性降低输出质量，且重写问题对查询有正向帮助。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学领域中智能体系统的规范化人类对齐评估提供了可复用模板，并验证了LLM裁判在专业场景中的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are reshaping scientific workflows in chemistry and drug discovery, but evaluating their open-ended, tool-augmented outputs remains a fundamental bottleneck. Reference-based metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE fail to capture semantic correctness, while expert human evaluation does not scale to the iteration speed these systems demand. The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative, but existing drug discovery benchmarks deploy LLM judges without validating their alignment with human experts. In this work, we present an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation framework for ChatInvent, an agentic drug discovery assistant deployed at AstraZeneca, with four contributions. First, we define four output-quality evaluation dimensions---Completeness, Relevancy, Structural Clarity, and Scope Adherence---alongside deterministic Tool Call Correctness checks. Second, we validate the judge through a human alignment study with five expert annotators, comparing Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, and Llama 3.1 70B as candidate judges. Third, we optimize the best-performing judge using few-shot demonstrations of human-annotated examples, improving alignment with the human majority vote from 0.80 to 0.86. Fourth, applying the optimized judge to 70 held-out questions, we surface concrete limitations and find that informal phrasings do not systematically degrade output quality; if anything, it is helpful to have the LLM rewrite the original question before querying the agent. Our framework provides a reusable template for human-aligned evaluation of agentic systems in scientific domains.

</details>


### [97] [Continuous-Time Quantum Walks based Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20738)
*Yuliang Zhan,Zefeng Gao,Jian Li,Yang Liu,Hao sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CTQW-GNN，利用连续时间量子游走的酉传播子同时缓解低通滤波偏差和过度平滑问题，并设计三个聚合模块以兼顾同/异嗜图性能，附理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在异嗜图上因消息传递的低通滤波特性表现差，且堆叠层导致过平滑；已有方法分别处理这些问题或依赖经验启发式，且部分过平滑缓解手段牺牲表达力。

Method: 基于CTQW传播子e^{-iHt}的酉性：保持高频信号以缓解低通偏差，维持特征范数和Dirichlet能量以避免过平滑；结合CTQW聚合（保留中高频）、CTQW注意力聚合（多跳邻居图）和低频GAT分支（保持同嗜图性能），并提供谱隙分析和Lieb-Robinson界指导时间t选择。

Result: 理论分析证明能量保持和游走时间选择规则，实验（原文未给出具体数值）预期在异嗜和同嗜图上均表现良好。

Conclusion: CTQW-GNN从理论上统一解决低通滤波和过平滑问题，为GNN设计提供量子行走视角的新范式。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used on graph-structured data, but most suffer from two key weaknesses. First, message passing behaves as a low-pass filter under the homophily assumption, leading to poor performance on heterophilic graphs. Second, stacking layers drives node features toward constants, causing over-smoothing. Existing methods usually address these issues separately, while the few joint solutions rely largely on empirical heuristics, and many over-smoothing remedies sacrifice model expressiveness.
  We propose \textbf{CTQW-GNN}, a GNN based on Continuous-Time Quantum Walks (CTQW), to address both issues with theoretical justification. Its design exploits two properties of the CTQW propagator $e^{-\mathrm{i}Ht}$. First, it is unitary and has eigenvalues on the unit circle, so no frequency component is damped, counteracting the low-pass bias. Second, unitarity preserves feature norms and prevents the Dirichlet energy from decaying exponentially with depth, thereby mitigating over-smoothing.
  CTQW-GNN combines three complementary aggregation modules. \textit{CTQW-based Aggregation} evolves node features through the unitary propagator, preserving mid- and high-frequency signals for heterophilic graphs while preventing Dirichlet-energy collapse. \textit{CTQW-Attention Aggregation} constructs a multi-hop neighbor graph from CTQW amplitudes and applies attention over it, enabling access to distant homophilic nodes missed by single-hop aggregation. \textit{LF Aggregation} uses a standard low-pass GAT branch to retain strong performance on homophilic graphs, where pure CTQW aggregation can be suboptimal. We further provide a spectral-gap analysis explaining energy preservation and a Lieb--Robinson-type bound that gives a principled rule for selecting the walk time $t$.

</details>


### [98] [TracingFlow: A Simulation-Free Trajectory Inference Framework Based on Second-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21070)
*Yuhao Sun,Zekun Wu,Zixun Huang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: TracingFlow 提出了一种基于 Flow Matching 的二阶动力学生成框架，通过神经网络回归加速度场，实现对动态最优加速度传输（DOAT）问题的精确高效求解，在稀疏时间快照下重建连续系统演化，优于传统一阶最优传输方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输框架局限于一阶动力学，假设无记忆速度场，无法捕捉细胞分化等过程中的调控动量与时间延迟响应，导致轨迹过度平滑、表达能力受限。

Method: 采用模拟自由的 Flow Matching 框架，扩展至二阶动力学；用神经网络回归加速度场，直接求解 DOAT 问题，学习底层力场以生成高曲率非线性演化轨迹。

Result: 在复杂合成数据和大型 scRNA-seq 数据集上，TracingFlow 在分布重建精度和轨迹保真度上优于一阶方法；整合谱系追踪先验后，恢复的动态结构兼具数学最优性与生物学合理性。

Conclusion: 二阶动力学建模有效突破了传统一阶 OT 的表达瓶颈，为单细胞组学等领域的连续演化推断提供了更强大且生物学可解释的工具。

Abstract: Inferring continuous system evolution from sparse temporal snapshots is a key challenge in generative modeling and single-cell omics. While Optimal Transport (OT) is popular, existing frameworks are largely restricted to first-order dynamics, assuming memoryless velocity fields. This limits expressiveness, as first-order systems fail to account for regulatory momentum and time-delayed responses inherent in processes like cell differentiation. Here, we introduce TracingFlow, a simulation-free Flow Matching framework generalizing to second-order dynamics. By using neural networks to regress the acceleration field, TracingFlow provides an exact, efficient solution to the Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) problem. Unlike first-order methods yielding over-smoothed trajectories, our second-order formulation captures high-curvature transitions and nonlinear evolutions by learning the underlying force fields. Evaluated on complex synthetic and large-scale scRNA-seq datasets, TracingFlow achieves superior accuracy in distributional reconstruction and trajectory faithfulness. Moreover, by integrating lineage tracing priors, it recovers dynamical structures that are both mathematically optimal and biologically plausible.

</details>


### [99] [Is Multimodal Speculative Decoding Ready for Diffusion-Based Parallel Drafting? A Survey and Empirical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20743)
*Yantao Li,Huanlin Gao,Fang Zhao,Chao Tan,Qiang Hui,Shuting Liu,Fuyuan Shi,Ting Lu,Shaoan Zhao,Xueqiang Guo,Xinpei Su,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过模态中心的综述和跨架构的实证研究，探讨了多模态推测解码是否已准备好采用基于扩散的并行草稿生成（如DFlash和DSpark），并提出了统一分类法和标准化基准下的性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块并行生成草稿在纯文本LLM中已实现显著加速（最高3.6倍），但在多模态模型中的适用性尚未被探索，现有工作多集中于输入压缩或适配器对齐，缺乏对并行草稿生成的研究。

Method: 结合模态中心调查与跨架构实证研究，覆盖视觉-语言、视频-语言、音频和VLA架构，从草稿并行性和跨模态信息交互双视角分析，提出隔离草稿端并行的统一分类法，并在OCR、VQA、视觉推理和图像描述等标准基准上进行实证比较。

Result: 现有方法在多模态场景下的并行草稿生成效果有限，实证比较揭示了不同并行度下的性能差异，并识别了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 多模态推测解码尚未完全适配扩散式并行草稿生成，仍需解决跨模态动态交互和并行效率的开放挑战，未来方向包括更高效的草稿架构和统一验证策略。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by allowing a lightweight drafter to propose future tokens while a target model verifies them in parallel. Its lossless guarantee has motivated a line of work that pushes the drafter itself toward parallel generation. The most recent paradigm is block-parallel generative drafting, including diffusion-based methods such as DFlash and DSpark, achieving up to 3.6x speedup on common daily chatting tasks. While this transition is well studied in text-only LLMs, its applicability to multimodal models remains an open question. Existing multimodal speculative decoding efforts focus on input compression, adapter alignment, candidate coverage, or modality-specific verification; however, block-parallel generative drafting remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, this paper combines a modality-centered survey with a cross-architecture empirical study to ask: Is multimodal speculative decoding ready for diffusion-based parallel drafting? In this survey, we systematically analyze a wide spectrum of multimodal models, spanning Vision-Language, Video-Language, Audio, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures, from the dual perspectives of drafting parallelism and cross-modal information interaction. We introduce a unified taxonomy that isolates drafter-side parallelism from orthogonal design choices such as tree construction and verification strategies. Furthermore, we provide a comprehensive empirical comparison of existing methods under varying degrees of parallelism across standardized multimodal benchmarks, including OCR, VQA, visual reasoning, and image captioning. Finally, we summarize the limitations of current approaches, discuss open challenges, and outline promising future directions for this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [100] [FlatLand: Personalized Graph Federated Learning via Tailored Lorentz Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21096)
*Jiahong Liu,Ram Samarth B B,Xinyu Fu,Menglin Yang,Weixi Zhang,Rex Ying,Irwin King*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FlatLand，一种在洛伦兹空间中嵌入客户端图数据的个性化联邦学习方法，通过参数解耦策略分离异构信息与共享知识，实现高效聚合，在低维设置下性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法忽略图结构的几何特性，难以处理客户端图数据高度异质的问题，而双曲几何天然适配图的负曲率特性，洛伦兹空间的类时维度可编码客户端差异性。

Method: 将客户端数据嵌入定制洛伦兹空间，利用双曲几何捕获图负曲率；设计参数解耦策略，将异构信息存入类时参数、共享知识存入类空参数，实现无需相似度估计的直接聚合。

Result: 在多种联邦图学习任务上，FlatLand达到优越性能，尤其在低维嵌入设置下表现突出。

Conclusion: 验证了双曲几何与洛伦兹空间在个性化联邦图学习中的有效性，为异构图数据联邦学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [101] [CAS: Conformalized Agentic Search via Adaptive Retrieval and Policy Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20771)
*Zixi Zhu,Jiayuan Su,Jian Zhang,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Conformalized Agentic Search（CAS）框架，在检索和训练两侧分别采用自适应预测集（APS）与自适应共形推断（ACI）建立可靠性保证，既减少证据丢失/噪声与幻觉，又通过GRPO惩罚低置信轨迹，显著提升问答准确率并减少冗余工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索Agent在RL微调中面临严重可靠性危机：启发式Top-K检索导致关键证据丢失或噪声混入，而渐进RL引起的过度自信造成幻觉答案和冗余检索。

Method: 在检索侧用APS将统计覆盖转化为动态文档截断，构造大小自适应的预测集；在训练侧用ACI动态构造可控覆盖的预测集以量化答案置信度，并将低置信轨迹的惩罚项融入GRPO目标。

Result: 在单跳和多跳QA数据集上，该方法显著提升推理准确率，并大幅减少冗余工具调用。

Conclusion: CAS确立了高可靠且高效的Agent范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Search Agents face a severe reliability crisis during reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning. Heuristic Top-K retrieval often causes critical evidence loss or noise inclusion, while over-confidence induced by progressive RL leads to hallucinated answers and redundant searches.
  To build highly reliable agents, we introduce Conformal Prediction (CP) and propose Conformalized Agentic Search (CAS). This framework establishes reliability guarantees on both the retrieval and training sides: on the retrieval side, an Adaptive Prediction Set (APS), a specific CP realization, translates statistical coverage into dynamic document truncation to construct prediction sets that are adaptive in size; on the training side, Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI), a dynamic CP algorithm, dynamically constructs prediction sets with controllable coverage to quantify answer confidence, which is then used to penalize low-confidence trajectories within the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective, ensuring the model learns only from reliable ones.
  Experiments across single-hop and multi-hop QA datasets demonstrate that our framework significantly improves reasoning accuracy while drastically reducing redundant tool invocations, establishing a highly reliable and efficient agent paradigm. Our code is available at https://github.com/S1llyBird/CAS.

</details>


### [102] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出COEC，一种无需训练的交替左右正交旋转补偿框架，通过校准Gram矩阵和广义交叉验证提升结构化剪枝后大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free补偿方法仅使用加法偏置或单一输出侧正交旋转，未改变保留权重的输入奇异帧，限制了列移除后的适应能力，导致精度损失。

Method: COEC在保留权重上交替应用左右正交旋转，右旋转在降维Stiefel流形上优化，用广义交叉验证选择每层正则化强度，校准Gram矩阵减少高能激活方向主导，并加入对齐惩罚保持相邻注意力投影的几何关系。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5系列上，COEC在所有模型上改善困惑度，并在大多数设置下提升零样本准确率，高稀疏度下增益更大。

Conclusion: 后剪枝补偿能部分恢复列移除造成的性能损失，COEC是一种有效且独立的补偿方法。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [103] [Knowing but Not Saying: Preventing Factual Access Failures in LLM SFT via Recall-Anchored Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20794)
*Haodong Chen,Yadong Wang,Shengtao Wen,Dong Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文发现SFT导致的事实退化并非完全源于知识遗忘，而是一种‘事实访问失败’：模型在受约束评估中能识别正确答案，但开放生成时失败。为此提出基座锚定蒸馏（RAD），通过对齐基座模型在无标注OOD文本上的软分布来恢复丢失的召回率，无需标注数据或外部评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT会损害模型在目标领域外的事实行为，但现有‘灾难性遗忘’解释不够精确。研究发现模型在多项选择等受限条件下仍能选对答案，而开放式生成失败，表明底层事实未必被擦除，问题可能出在生成通路或表达层面。

Method: RAD是一种基座锚定的自蒸馏目标：在无标注OOD文本上，让微调模型拟合原始基座模型的软延续分布，从而保留分布外生成行为。它不需要金标准OOD答案、外部评判或标注事实数据，仅利用基座模型自身的概率输出作为监督信号。

Result: 在三个基座模型上使用MedMCQA微调后，RAD能稳定恢复部分丢失的OOD召回率，同时保持目标域适应性能。与简单地回放相同OOD文本相比，RAD效果更好，证明关键保留信号是基座模型的软分布而非单纯文本曝光。

Conclusion: SFT导致的事实退化包含真实错误生成与表达层面失败（如冗长、格式不符、精确匹配伪影）。RAD通过基座软分布锚定有效缓解了这一问题，为保留分布外事实能力提供了一种无需额外标注数据的轻量方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can degrade factual behavior outside the target domain. This degradation is often described as catastrophic forgetting, yet open-ended factual failures do not necessarily imply that the underlying facts have been erased. In this work, we identify a more specific phenomenon, factual access failure: after domain SFT, models can still recognize or rank the correct answer under constrained evaluation, while failing to produce it in closed-book generation. Through benchmark-level comparisons, same-fact multiple-choice and generation probes, and failure-mode analysis, we show that SFT-induced factual degradation reflects both genuine wrong-answer generations and expression-level failures such as verbosity, formatting mismatch, and exact-match artifacts. To address this problem, we introduce Recall-Anchored Distillation (RAD), a base-anchored self-distillation objective that preserves out-of-distribution generation behavior by aligning the adapted model with the original base model's soft continuation distribution on unlabeled OOD text. RAD requires no gold OOD answers, external judges, or labeled factual data. Across three backbones fine-tuned on MedMCQA, RAD recovers a consistent portion of the lost OOD recall while preserving target-domain adaptation. Compared with replay on the same OOD text, RAD shows that the key preservation signal is the base model's soft distribution rather than additional text exposure alone.

</details>


### [104] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NSPIN神经符号框架，从非结构化临床叙述中诱导概率规划领域模型，在腹腔镜阑尾切除数据上验证了泛化性和临床一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除术等外科流程复杂且风险高，但缺乏结构化事件数据，临床叙述中隐含动作普遍存在，现有经验符号方法和LLM均无法单独解决建模挑战。

Method: NSPIN利用预训练LLM从原始文本提取和补全结构化事件序列，诱导PPDDL模型，并通过LLM提出条件修订、结合经验验证进行精化。

Result: 在9名外科医生书写的2660份手术记录上，NSPIN生成的模型能泛化到未见记录，专家临床审查显示诱导知识大体符合手术实践。

Conclusion: NSPIN为临床叙述中概率规划模型诱导提供了可行方案，弥合了符号方法与LLM的互补优势，并展现出实际临床决策支持潜力。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic Context Scheduling: Learning Beyond the Static Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20799)
*Martin Mráz,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出将动态上下文调度作为情境强化学习的训练工具，通过预定义调度在训练回合内演化环境上下文，以提升策略的泛化能力。实验表明，动态调度在分布外（OOD）场景下匹配或超越静态基线，并在复杂环境中提升分布内（ID）性能；自动搜索多阶段课程可媲美网格搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情境强化学习通常将回合内上下文变化视为部署时的不确定性，而本文认为这种变化可作为受控的训练塑形机制，帮助策略探索更丰富和时序结构化的环境参数空间，从而提升泛化能力。

Method: 提出DYNAMICCARLENV框架，封装情境环境并集成可插拔调度族（如正弦偏移、余弦退火），在训练回合内按预定调度演化上下文；同时探索自动搜索多阶段课程以发现更优调度。

Result: 在CartPole、BipedalWalker和VehicleRacing（使用CARL情境化）上，动态调度在OOD场景下匹配或优于静态基线，并在复杂环境（BipedalWalker和VehicleRacing）中取得更高的ID性能；自动多阶段课程搜索达到与广泛网格搜索相当的性能。

Conclusion: 动态上下文调度是一种有效且可自动化的训练策略，能增强情境强化学习的泛化能力，尤其在复杂环境中，为未来设计更智能的上下文调度方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: We study dynamic context scheduling as a training instrument for contextual re- inforcement learning. Rather than treating intra-episode context variation as a deployment reality, we treat it as a controlled shaping mechanism. Thereby, context evolves within each training episode according to a predetermined schedule, expos- ing the policy to a richer and more temporally structured region of the environment parameter space. We introduce DYNAMICCARLENV, a framework that wraps contextual environments with pluggable schedule families, such as sinusoidal off- sets or cosine annealing. Across CartPole, BipedalWalker and VehicleRacing with CARL contextualization, we show that dynamic schedules match or outperform static context baselines in the out-of-distribution (OOD) regimes. Interestingly, for the more complex BipedalWalker and VehicleRacing environments we also achieve higher in-distribution (ID) evaluation performance. Preliminary findings indicate that automatic search for multi-stage curricula can successfully discover schedules that improve generalization, performing comparably to extensive grid search over single-stage schedulers.

</details>


### [106] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Tydra，一种混合Transformer-SSM架构的表格基础模型，通过交错注意力与SSM层，在30个OpenML数据集上比TabPFN推理快30%且保持性能，并优于大10倍的Hydra模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类表格基础模型（如TabPFN）性能强但计算成本随上下文长度二次增长，而SSM替代方案（如Hydra）牺牲精度换效率，需要兼顾两者。

Method: Tydra采用混合架构，交错堆叠注意力层和SSM层，用于表格上下文学习，平衡全局依赖建模与线性复杂度。

Result: 在30个OpenML数据集上，推理时间比TabPFN降低30%，预测性能基本保持；同时比约大10倍的Hydra模型性能更好且推理更快。

Conclusion: 混合架构是表格基础模型的有前景方向，可在效率与准确性之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [107] [SPARC: Single-Pass Scaling for Motion Forecasting with Conformal Bayesian Last Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20802)
*Sakif Hossain,Julian Teusch,Jörg P. Müller*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: SPARC是一种用于运动预测的贝叶斯-共形不确定性层，通过共轭贝叶斯最后一层和结构化因子分解，在不改变轨迹协方差结构的情况下提供解析的逐视界认知不确定性标度，并实现有限样本有效的校准预测管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测器在准确性和速度上表现良好，但可靠部署需要结构化、校准且高效的不确定性估计；贝叶斯和集成方法需重复随机推断，共形校准单独缺乏认知信号且不保留轨迹协方差结构。

Method: 确定性MLP主干预测未来均值，共轭贝叶斯最后一层将时域特征杠杆转化为解析的逐视界认知标度κ_t(x)，该标度膨胀图-时间高斯协方差而不改变其相关结构，再通过分裂共形校准生成95%边际预测管；关键接口为结构化因子分解κ_t(x)Σ_str,t(x)，无需蒙特卡洛采样即可将特征空间认知不确定性注入轨迹密度。

Result: 在九个数据集-协议块及确定性、多模态和校准基线上，SPARC在NLL和MPJPE+NLL组合标准上排名第一，同时保持有竞争力的点准确性和高效校准管；按κ排序窗口可分离高误差案例。

Conclusion: SPARC提供了一种轻量级的风险监测工具，其认知标度可有效识别高误差情况，在不确定性质量与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Human motion forecasters are increasingly accurate and fast, but reliable deployment requires uncertainty estimates that are structured, calibrated, and efficient. Bayesian and ensemble-based uncertainty estimates often require repeated stochastic inference [15, 26], while conformal calibration alone does not provide an epistemic signal or preserve trajectory covariance structure [14, 50]. We introduce SPARC (Single-Pass Adaptive Risk Calibration), a Bayesian-conformal uncertainty layer for motion forecasting. A deterministic MLP backbone predicts the future mean, and a conjugate Bayesian last layer converts time-domain feature leverage into an analytic horizon-wise epistemic scale $κ_t(x)$. This scale inflates a graph-temporal Gaussian covariance without changing its correlation structure, and split conformal calibration produces 95% marginal prediction tubes with finite-sample validity under exchangeability. The key interface is the structured factorization $κ_t(x)Σ_{\mathrm{str},t}(x)$, which injects feature-space epistemic uncertainty into trajectory densities without Monte Carlo sampling. Across nine dataset-protocol blocks and deterministic, multimodal, and calibration baselines, SPARC ranks first on NLL and on the combined MPJPE+NLL criterion while retaining competitive point accuracy and efficient calibrated tubes. Ranking windows by $κ$ separates high-error cases, making the scale usable as a lightweight risk monitor.

</details>


### [108] [Prediction certification cannot replace explanation certification: a competence envelope for trustworthy AI under compound stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20825)
*Nataliya Shakhovska,Ivan Izonin,Stergios-Aristoteles Mitoulis*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文证明仅基于预测的模型认证（如准确率、校准和覆盖）不足以确保模型可信度，并提出结合预测与解释认证的“能力包络”框架以检测隐藏失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统认证依赖预测侧指标，但其是否足以建立信任尚不明确；本文旨在揭示预测侧认证的局限性并寻求更全面的认证方法。

Method: 通过分离定理证明可靠与受损模型在预测侧证书下可能完全一致，但解释保真度和部署行为不同；提出能力包络框架，将预测和解释认证结合为统一标准，并在多数据集和模型类上验证。

Result: 能力包络框架能揭示预测侧认证无法捕获的失败模式，且要求访问模型决策机制而非仅输出。

Conclusion: 要认证预测行为中不可见的失败，需结合模型输出与决策机制的证据；仅依赖预测侧性能不足以保证可信度。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly make consequential judgments - which patient is deteriorating, which building is safe to enter, whether an image is authentic and are trusted on the strength of how accurately and confidently they predict. The safeguards that certify them are correspondingly prediction-based: accuracy, calibration and conformal coverage all measure how well a model performs. Whether such checks are sufficient to establish model trustworthiness has remained unclear. Here we prove that they cannot. We establish a separation theorem showing that a reliable model and a compromised one can be identical under every prediction-side certificate, including accuracy, calibration and coverage, yet differ arbitrarily in explanation fidelity and deployment behaviour. Detecting this failure requires access to the model's decision mechanism in addition to its predictions. We introduce the competence envelope as an operational framework that combines prediction and explanation certification into a single deployable criterion. Across diverse datasets and model classes, the proposed framework reveals failure modes that prediction-side certification alone does not capture. Certification against failures that are invisible in prediction behaviour therefore requires evidence about the model's decision mechanism as well as its outputs.

</details>


### [109] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出E²-TTT，通过闭式状态转移精确复现逐token递归的权重和动量更新，实现并行化训练，兼顾表达力与硬件效率，在语言建模和上下文检索上表现优异，并显著提升长度外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTT方法在逐token更新的表达力与块级近似的硬件效率之间存在矛盾，需要一种既能保持更新动态的时序结构又能完全并行化的方法。

Method: 在块起始权重处取梯度的标准近似下，推导出闭式状态转移，精确匹配块末的快速权重和动量状态，从而实现块级并行训练而不丢失逐token递归的时序结构。

Result: 训练至1.3B参数，语言建模性能与现有TTT和混合注意力基线相当，上下文检索更优；在长达8倍训练长度的外推测试中保持90%以上准确率，训练吞吐量与高效块级方法匹配。

Conclusion: E²-TTT有效调和了表达力与效率，为长上下文处理提供了实用的高性能方案。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [110] [RAG Deserves an Index: Why Ingest-Time Compilation Beats Query-Time Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20845)
*Kyle Wild,Yusuke Takahashi,Asako Uraki*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出“ ingest-time semantic compilation (ISC)”范式，主张在写入时将语料语义编译为可查询的索引结构（增量维护的嵌入+经校验的原子声明），以应对检索增强问答中推理开销随上下文膨胀的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在每次查询时重复推导语料含义，产生高昂推理成本；模型价格下降但上下文增长更快，导致总开销上升，类似数据库全表扫描问题。

Method: ISC将语料编译为双层基质：增量维护的嵌入层与编译时验证来源的原子声明层，并视其为一级数据库对象，具备DDL、维护/迁移契约和成本模型。

Result: 增量更新比全量重建便宜33.7倍且精度一致；在500个访谈转录样本中，编译声明在全部32个预算-模型组合中胜出（85.2%正确率，2.2k tokens vs 最佳分块72.5%，16.3k tokens），唯一持平的是混合检索+重排的上下文分块管道，但需约21倍查询token。

Conclusion: ISC是解决RAG扩展性问题的有效方向，且当前最佳基线本质上已部分实现编译；未来需开展编译规划器、读取规划等系统性研究。

Abstract: Nearly every retrieval-augmented question-answering system in production ships with a hidden interpreter: on each query a language model re-derives the meaning of raw corpus text and then throws that work away. Cheaper models do not close the gap: per-token prices have fallen by orders of magnitude while inference spend has risen, because context volume grows faster than prices fall. This is the modern equivalent of the full-table scan, and the remedy is the one databases found fifty years ago: do the expensive work once, at write time, into a maintained structure that makes reads cheap. A corpus whose read pattern is known before it ever meets a user can and should be indexed too.
  We call the paradigm ingest-time semantic compilation (ISC): compile a corpus's meaning into a queryable substrate with two coupled layers - incrementally maintained embeddings, and atomic claims whose provenance is validated at compile time - and treat that substrate as a first-class database object with its own DDL, maintenance contract, migration contract, and cost model.
  Two existence proofs support it. Substrate upkeep scales with change rather than corpus size: incremental updates run 33.7x cheaper than reconstruction while tracking it to floating-point precision. And on a held-out sample of 500 broadcast-interview transcripts, compiled claims as the retrieval payload win all 32 budget-by-model cells: 85.2% correct from roughly 2.2k reader tokens against 72.5% from 16.3k for the best chunk configuration anywhere. The only baseline that keeps pace is a contextualized-chunk pipeline with hybrid retrieval and reranking, statistically indistinguishable from compiled claims at roughly twenty-one times the query-path tokens - and it reaches that parity, we argue, precisely because it has itself begun to compile. We close with the systems agenda this opens, from compilation planners to read planning.

</details>


### [111] [ReCurveflow: A Flow Matching Framework that Learns Curved Reaction Trajectories to Predict Transition State Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20869)
*Seungheun Baek,Mogan Gim,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ReCurveflow框架，利用连续弯曲参考路径和离路径校正学习预测化学反应过渡态几何，在多数指标上优于七个基线，并开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的TS预测方法监督直线路径，与实际反应轨迹不符，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 采用流匹配框架，从完整NEB能带插值连续弯曲参考路径作为监督信号，并引入离路径校正机制，在推理滚动时产生修正速度场以抵抗曝光偏差。

Result: 在三种数据划分和六项评估指标上，ReCurveflow在大多数组合中取得最佳结果；定性分析显示生成轨迹的能量曲线接近参考NEB路径，初始化解加速NEB优化，速度场呈现预期校正行为。

Conclusion: ReCurveflow通过弯曲路径监督和离路径校正显著提升TS预测精度与鲁棒性，且代码公开可复现。

Abstract: Predicting transition states (TS) in chemical reactions is crucial, as they provide insights into reaction mechanisms. Recent work on TS prediction have focused on flow matching supervised on straight linear paths that do not align with actual reaction trajectories. We propose a novel flow matching-based framework ReCurveflow that learns to predict TS geometries supervised on continuously curved reference paths interpolated from a full NEB-derived band of molecular geometries. We also introduce off-path correction, which grants ReCurveflow with the ability to produce corrective velocity fields when engaged off-path geometry states during inference rollout, leading to better resistance against exposure bias and accuracy in TS prediction. Across three data splits and six evaluation metrics, ReCurveflow achieves the best result on the majority of split-metric combinations against seven baselines. Qualitative analyses further show that ReCurveflow generates reaction trajectories with energy profiles that closely track the reference NEB path, provides initializations that ease the NEB optimization bottleneck, and exhibits the intended corrective behavior in its learned velocity fields. The ReCurveflow codebase is publicly available at https://github.com/dmis-lab/ReCurveflow.

</details>


### [112] [Graph-Operator World Models for Morphology-Parameter Generalization in Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20936)
*Xu Yang,Yiqin Yang,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GraphOp-WM，通过图结构世界模型将形态无关的动态基与形态相关的算子分离，实现对未见形态参数的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续控制世界模型针对固定物理系统训练，当形态参数变化时性能退化；仅提供参数作为条件信息未明确哪些转移可复用、哪些应随形态变化。

Method: 将机器人表示为属性图，将转移分解为形态无关的局部动态基和形态条件化的结构化算子；算子包含节点局部调制、运动树耦合和低秩全局修正，并通过信息分离、基归一化和成对形态监督来强化形态依赖集中于算子通路；图级读出和边动作表示兼容奖励、价值和TD-MPC规划。

Result: 在MuJoCo的Hopper、Walker2d、HalfCheetah上定义插值、外推和组合泛化参数划分，验证了图算子世界模型对未见形态的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GraphOp-WM通过显式结构化分解实现了跨形态参数的高效泛化，为连续控制世界模型提供了可扩展的架构设计。

Abstract: World models for continuous control are commonly trained for a fixed physical system and can degrade when known morphology parameters such as link lengths, masses, damping, and actuation change. Existing approaches often provide these parameters as conditioning information, but leave unspecified which part of the learned transition should remain reusable and which part should change with morphology. We propose Graph-Operator World Models (GraphOp-WM), a structured world model for generalization across unseen morphology parameters within related articulated robot families. GraphOp-WM represents bodies and their kinematic relations as an attributed graph and factorizes each transition into a morphology-independent local dynamics basis and a morphology-conditioned structured operator. The operator combines node-local modulation, kinematic-tree coupling, and a low-rank global correction, while architectural information separation, basis normalization, and paired-morphology supervision encourage static morphology dependence to be carried by the operator pathway. Graph-level readout and edge-wise action representations provide a compatible interface for reward, value, and TD-MPC-style planning. We further define controlled MuJoCo parameter splits covering interpolation, extrapolation, and held-out compositions of link geometry, mass, damping, and actuation parameters in Hopper, Walker2d, and HalfCheetah.

</details>


### [113] [TLive-Omni: An Omni-Modal Understanding Model for E-Commerce Live Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20958)
